'campaign_name',\n    'active_clinics',\n    'drug_cost_per_1000_doses_usd',\n    'doses_per_day',\n    'crate_tier',\n    'crate_cost_usd',\n    'payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd',\n    with (ROOT / name).open(newline='', encoding='utf-8') as handle:\n        return list(csv.DictReader(handle))\n\n\ndef load_manifest_campaigns(path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    with   for row in rows\n        if row.get('suspension_status', '').strip() == 'hold'\n    }\n\n\ndef build_crate_costs(rows: Iterable[Dict[str, str]]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    return {\n campaign: Dict[str, Any], rows: Iterable[Dict[str, str]]\n) -> Dict[str, str]:\n    labels = {str(campaign.get('campaign_name', '')).strip()}\n    aliases = campaign.get('alias_labels') or []\n    if isinstance(aliases, list):\n        labels.update(str(alias).strip() for alias in aliases)\n    labels.discard('')\n\n    matches = [\n        row\n        for row in rows\n        if {campaign.get('campaign_id')}\")\n    return max(matches, key=lambda row: row.get('cycle_tag', '').strip())\n\n\ndef build_active_clinic_overrides(rows: Iterable[Dict[str, str]]) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    chosen: Dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}\n    for row in rows:\n        if row.get('state', '').strip() != 'approved':\n            continue\n        if not non_empty(row.get('revision')) or not     revenue = payment * active_clinics * dispatches_per_year\n        drug_cost = (\n            drug_cost_per_1000\n            * active_clinics\n            * doses_per_day\n            * days_per_dispatch\n            * dispatches_per_year\n            / 1000\n        )\n        # Crates are needed for every active clinic on every dispatch, so the\n        # tier cost is per clinic per dispatch rather than one crate per campaign.\n        crate_cost = crate_cost_usd * active_clinics * dispatches_per_year\n        margin = revenue - drug_cost - crate_cost\n        return {\n            'revenue': revenue,\n            'drug_cost': drug_cost,\n            'crate_cost': crate_cost,\n            'margin': margin,\n        }\n\n    six_day = difference = twelve_day['margin'] - six_day['margin']\n\n    result = {\n        'campaign_id': str(campaign['campaign_id']),\n        'campaign_name': str(campaign['campaign_name']),\n 'annual_crate_cost_12_day_usd': currency(twelve_day['crate_cost']),\n        'annual_revenue_6_day_usd': currency(six_day['revenue']),\n        'annual_revenue_12_day_usd': 'annual_margin_difference_12_minus_6_usd': currency(difference),\n    }\n    return {field: result[field] for field in CAMPAIGN_FIELDS}\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    campaigns =
